  try {
    if (window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone) return;
    window.__mintlifyInitialSidebarScrollDone = true;

    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    var candidates = [];
    if (path) candidates.push(path);
    if (path.startsWith('/')) candidates.push(path.slice(1));
    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }
    }
    if (!parent) return;

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="description"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#description" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Description</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Create, update, or manage a hyperparameter sweep.</span>
<span data-as="p">Provide a YAML config file to create a sweep. Define the search
strategy, parameters, and metric to optimize in the config.
Register the sweep with the W&amp;B server and print the sweep ID
and a command to start an agent.</span>
<span data-as="p">Provide a sweep ID (or full path entity/project/sweep_id) with a
state flag (<code>--stop</code>, <code>--cancel</code>, <code>--pause</code>, or <code>--resume</code>) to manage
an existing sweep.</span>
<span data-as="p">The sweep ID is a unique identifier for the sweep, generated by
W&amp;B when the sweep is created. You can find the sweep ID in the W&amp;B App
or in the output of the <code>wandb sweep</code> command when you create a new sweep.</span>
<span data-as="p">For example, to create a sweep using the configuration defined in
sweep_config.yaml. Use the current user’s default entity and project:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 users expect in-site navigation to stay in the same tab.\n * - The theme may show an external-link arrow on all nav items; local links should\n *   show a chevron instead to signal in-site navigation.\n * - This script only changes links that match the \"group flex\" nav pattern and\n *   have a local href; external (http/https) links are left unchanged.\n *\n * How it works:\n * - Runs on DOMContentLoaded and at several delayed intervals (for slow-hydrating nav).\n * - Uses a MutationObserver to run whenever the DOM gains new nodes or a link's\n *   target attribute changes, so links are fixed as soon as the framework\n *   renders or re-applies target=\"_blank\".\n * - Runs on pageshow so that when the user navigates back, links are fixed again.\n * - Selects \u003ca\u003e elements whose class list contains both \"group\" and \"flex\"\n *   (the Mintlify sidebar nav link pattern).\n * - For each such link with a local href: removes the target attribute and\n *   replaces the arrow SVG with a chevron SVG.\n *\n * Environment:\n * - Loaded globally by Mintlify for every docs page (any .js in the content dir).\n * - Wrapped in an IIFE to avoid polluting the global scope.\n */\n\n(function () {\n  /**\n   * Determines whether an href should be treated as local (same-origin / in-doc).\n   * Local links should open in the same tab; external links may keep target=\"_blank\".\n   *\n   * @param {string | null | undefined} href - The href value from the anchor (e.g. getAttribute('href')).\n   * @returns {boolean} - True if the link is local and safe to open in the same tab.\n   */\n  function isLocalHref(href) {\n    // Missing or empty href: treat as local (e.g. placeholder or JS-handled link).\n    if (!href || typeof href !== 'string') return true;\n\n    var trimmed = href.trim();\n    // Fragment-only links (e.g. #section) are in-page and always local.\n    if (trimmed === '' || trimmed.startsWith('#')) return true;\n\n    // Compare case-insensitively so HTTP: and https: are both treated as external.\n    var lower = trimmed.toLowerCase();\n    // Local: relative paths (/foo), path-only, or protocol-relative that we treat as same-site.\n    // External: explicit http: or https: (and we do not change those).\n    return !lower.startsWith('http:') \u0026\u0026 !lower.startsWith('https:');\n  }\n\n  /** Chevron path matching Mintlify's sidebar group chevron (ChevronRightIcon, 18x18). */\n  var CHEVRON_PATH = 'M6.5 2.75L12.75 9L6.5 15.25';\n\n  /**\n   * Classes matching Mintlify's own sidebar group chevron (DropdownArrowIcon):\n   * its base ChevronRightIcon classes plus the sizing overrides the sidebar\n   * adds (w-2 wins over size-3 in the compiled CSS, giving the 8px width).\n   */\n  var CHEVRON_CLASS = 'size-3 transition-transform text-gray-400 group-hover:text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-600 dark:group-hover:text-gray-400 shrink-0 w-2 h-[1lh] -mr-0.5';\n\n  /**\n   * Replaces the arrow (external-link) icon with a chevron on a sidebar nav link.\n   * Matches the chevron Mintlify renders on expandable sidebar groups.\n   *\n   * @param {HTMLAnchorElement} a - The nav link element.\n   */\n  function swapArrowToChevron(a) {\n    // Only sidebar entries get the swap. current set renders with viewBox=\"0 0 18 18\".\n    // The trailing external-link arrow is the last such svg in the row (a\n    // leading item icon, if any, comes first), so match by position rather\n    // than the arrow's path geometry - the geometry has changed upstream\n    // before and silently broke this swap (DOCS-3053).\n    var svgs = a.querySelectorAll('svg[viewBox=\"0 0 18 18\"]');\n    if (!svgs.length) return;\n\n    var svg = svgs[svgs.length - 1];\n    if (svg.getAttribute('data-chevron') === '1') return;\n\n    var paths = svg.querySelectorAll('path');\n    if 'currentColor');\n      paths[i].setAttribute('stroke-width', '2');\n      paths[i].setAttribute('stroke-linecap', 'round');\n      paths[i].setAttribute('stroke-linejoin', 'round');\n    }\n    svg.setAttribute('data-chevron', '1');\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Finds all \"group flex\" nav links with a local href, removes target=\"_blank\"\n   * so they open in the same tab, and swaps the arrow icon Clicking such a tab\n *   opens the dropdown but performs no navigation — users expect a click to\n *   take them somewhere, and a hover or keyboard interaction to expose items.\n * - This script intercepts clicks on those dropdown tabs and navigates to\n *   the href of the first link in the dropdown.\n *\n * How it works:\n * - A MutationObserver watches the DOM for any Radix dropdown menu opening\n *   (via hover, click, or keyboard). Non-dropdown tabs (plain\n *   anchors) are untouched.\n *\n * Environment:\n * - Loaded globally by Mintlify for every docs page (any .js in the content\n *   dir is included).\n * - Wrapped in an IIFE to avoid polluting the global scope.\n */\n\n(function () {\n  /** Map from trigger label (e.g. \"Products\") to the first dropdown href. */\n  var firstHrefByLabel = Object.create(null);\n\n  /**\n   * Whether an element is a Mintlify dropdown-tab trigger button.\n   * @param {Element | null} el\n   * @returns {boolean}\n   */\n  function isDropdownTab(el) {\n    if (!el || el.tagName !== 'BUTTON') return false;\n    if (!el.classList || !el.classList.contains('nav-tabs-item')) return false;\n    return el.getAttribute('aria-haspopup') === 'menu';\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Stable key for a dropdown tab trigger. Safe to call repeatedly.\n   */\n  function cacheOpenMenus() {\n    var menus = document.querySelectorAll('[role=\"menu\"][data-state=\"open\"]');\n    for (var i = 0; i \u003c menus.length; i++) {\n      var menu = menus[i];\n      var labelledBy = menu.getAttribute('aria-labelledby');\n      if (!labelledBy) continue;\n      var btn = document.getElementById(labelledBy);\n      if (!isDropdownTab(btn)) continue;\n      var href = firstHrefIn(menu);\n      if (href) firstHrefByLabel[keyFor(btn)] = href;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Returns the currently-open dropdown menu triggered by the given button,\n   * or null if the menu is closed / not yet rendered.\n   * @param {HTMLElement} btn\n   * @returns {Element | null}\n   */\n  function openMenuFor(btn) {\n    if (!btn.id) return null;\n    // CSS.escape guards against odd characters in Radix-generated ids.\n    var safeId = (typeof CSS !== 'undefined' \u0026\u0026 CSS.escape) ? CSS.escape(btn.id) : btn.id;\n    return document.querySelector('[role=\"menu\"][aria-labelledby=\"' + safeId + '\"][data-state=\"open\"]');\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Click interceptor. Runs in capture phase so it can preempt Radix's own\n   * click handler when we already know where to go.\n   * @param {MouseEvent} e\n   */\n  function onClick(e) {\n    // Only act on primary-button clicks without modifier keys — let users\n    // still open the menu with middle/right click or keyboard if they want.\n    if (e.button !== 0 || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey) return;\n\n    var target = e.target;\n    var btn = target \u0026\u0026 target.closest ? target.closest('button.nav-tabs-item') : null;\n    if (!isDropdownTab(btn)) return;\n\n    var href = firstHrefByLabel[keyFor(btn)];\n    if (href) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      e.stopPropagation();\n      e.stopImmediatePropagation();\n      window.location.href = href;\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Not cached yet — let Radix open the menu, then navigate as soon as\n    // the first link is in the DOM. The URL #slug hash only\n *      deep-links within a single page and is dropped on navigation.\n *\n * Content tabs are the dominant language-switch pattern in these docs (hundreds\n * of \u003cTab title=\"Python\"\u003e/\u003cTab title=\"TypeScript\"\u003e), so without this the reader\n * has to re-pick their language on every page. This script gives both systems\n * unified, case-insensitive, cross-page persistence.\n *\n * How it works:\n * - Remember: a capture-phase pointerdown/click listener stores the chosen\n *   tab's label in localStorage when the label is an SDK language (see\n *   LANGUAGES — only Python/TypeScript). The whitelist stops both descriptive\n *   tabs (\"Web App\", \"Summary metrics\") and incidental code tabs (\"Bash\",\n *   \"cURL\") from clobbering the saved language.\n * - Restore: on load, after hydration, and on SPA route changes (detected by\n *   a MutationObserver watching location.pathname), any tab whose label\n *   matches the saved language (compared case-insensitively) is selected.\n *   Tabs already active are skipped, so this coexists with Mintlify's own\n *   \u003cCodeGroup\u003e restore without fighting it.\n * - Yield: when the reader manually picks a non-language tab (\"HTTP API\",\n *   \"Command Line\", …), its group is marked overridden and restore leaves it\n *   alone for the rest of that page view. Restore also only re-runs when the\n *   pathname actually changes, never on arbitrary DOM mutations — otherwise\n *   the mutation caused by the reader's own tab switch would re-trigger a\n *   restore that instantly snaps mixed groups (e.g. Python / TypeScript /\n *   HTTP API) back to the saved language, making other tabs unselectable.\n *\n * Selection uses a real pointer sequence, not element.click(): Mintlify's tabs\n * activate on pointer/mouse-down, and a synthetic click() does not switch them.\n *\n * Depends only on the ARIA tab contract Mintlify renders (role=\"tab\", with\n * data-state=\"active\" for code groups or aria-selected=\"true\" for content\n * tabs), so it is resilient to theme class-name churn.\n *\n * Environment:\n * - Loaded globally by Mintlify for every docs page (any .js in the content\n *   root is injected — see .mintignore). Wrapped in an IIFE.\n */\n\n(function () {\n  var STORAGE_KEY = 'wandb.docs.codeLanguage';\n\n  /**\n   * The SDK languages whose choice should follow the reader from page to page,\n   * compared lower-cased. Deliberately limited to Python and TypeScript — the\n   * two W\u0026B SDK languages and the only genuine cross-page preference.\n   *\n   * Incidental code tabs (Bash, shell, cURL, YAML, JSON, …) are intentionally\n   * NOT listed: persisting them would let a click on, say, a Bash tab overwrite\n   * the saved Python/TypeScript choice, and a later page whose tabs are only\n   * Python/TypeScript has no Bash match — so it would silently fall back to its\n   * first tab, defeating the persistence. Code groups use\n   * data-state=\"active\"; content tabs use aria-selected=\"true\".\n   * @param {Element} tab\n   * @returns {boolean}\n   */\n  function isActive(tab) {\n    return tab.getAttribute('data-state') === 'active' ||\n           tab.getAttribute('aria-selected') === 'true';\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * The tab group a tab belongs to — its ARIA tablist, or failing that its\n   * parent element. Bound to pointerdown (when tabs actually select) and click (covers\n   * keyboard activation). Only trusted events count — the synthetic clicks\n   * restore() dispatches must not register as reader choices.\n   * @param {Event} e\n   */\n  function onSelect(e) {\n    if (!e.isTrusted) return;\n    var tab = e.target \u0026\u0026 e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[role=\"tab\"]') : null;\n    if (!tab) return;\n    var label = labelOf(tab);\n    if (!label) return;\n    var group = overridden \u0026\u0026 groupOf(tab);\n    if (isLanguage(label)) {\n      write(label);\n      if (group) overridden.delete(group);\n    } else if (group) {\n      overridden.add(group);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Selects the saved language in every group that isn't already on it,\n   * except groups the reader has manually overridden this page view.\n   * Self-terminating: tabs already active are skipped, so the synthetic clicks\n   * we trigger here do not cause an observe/click feedback loop, and we never\n   * fight Mintlify's own already-applied \u003cCodeGroup\u003e selection.\n   */\n  function restore() {\n    var want = read();\n    if (!want) return;\n    want = want.toLowerCase();\n    var tabs = document.querySelectorAll('[role=\"tab\"]');\n    for (var i = 0; i \u003c tabs.length; i++) {\n      var tab = tabs[i];\n      if (labelOf(tab).toLowerCase() !== want || isActive(tab)) continue;\n      if (overridden) {\n        var group = groupOf(tab);\n        if (group \u0026\u0026 overridden.has(group)) continue;\n      }\n      activate(tab);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Debounce restore() into a single run after a burst of DOM mutations.\n  var pending = false;\n  function schedule() {\n    if (pending) return;\n    pending = true;\n    setTimeout(function () { pending = false; restore(); }, 80);\n  }\n\n  /** Restore now-ish, then again while late-hydrating content settles. */\n  function restoreSoon() {\n    schedule();\n    [300, 800, 1500].forEach(function (ms) { setTimeout(restore, ms); });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Mutation handler: re-applies the saved language only when the mutation\n   * burst accompanies an actual client-side navigation (the pathname\n   * changed). Restoring on every mutation would treat the reader's own tab\n   * switch — itself a DOM mutation — as a cue to snap the group back to the\n   * saved language.\n   */\n  var lastPathname = location.pathname;\n  function onMutate() {\n    if (location.pathname === lastPathname) return;\n    lastPathname = location.pathname;\n    restoreSoon();\n  }\n\n  function start() {\n    // Capture phase so we observe the selection around Mintlify's own handling.\n    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', onSelect, true);\n    document.addEventListener('click', onSelect, true);\n\n    // Initial load: restore immediately, then catch tabs that hydrate later.\n    restore();\n    restoreSoon();\n\n    // Re-apply on client-side route changes.\n    if (document.body) {\n      new MutationObserver(onMutate).observe(document.body, {\n        childList: true,\n        subtree: true\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Re-apply after back/forward (bfcache) restores.\n    window.addEventListener('pageshow', restore);\n  }\n\n  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {\n    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', start);\n  } else {\n    start();\n  }\n})();\n"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"53:[\"$\",\"$L55\",null,{\"customJsDisabled\":false,\"jsFiles\":[{\"_id\":\"69b863e17ba705a281faca09\",\"filePath\":\"analytics.js\",\"subdomain\":\"wb-21fd5541\",\"__v\":0,\"content\":\"$56\"},{\"_id\":\"69c6aefa7ba705a281717acd\",\"filePath\":\"local-links-same-tab.js\",\"subdomain\":\"wb-21fd5541\",\"__v\":0,\"content\":\"$57\"},{\"_id\":\"69efa08124b96b26b4805d10\",\"subdomain\":\"wb-21fd5541\",\"filePath\":\"dropdown-tabs-navigate.js\",\"__v\":0,\"content\":\"$58\"},{\"_id\":\"6a319d4f7b8d8de9e6aeac37\",\"filePath\":\"code-group-language-persist.js\",\"subdomain\":\"wb-21fd5541\",\"__v\":0,\"content\":\"$59\"},{\"_id\":\"6a57d81981eda2c0542f8cf5\",\"subdomain\":\"wb-21fd5541\",\"filePath\":\"kapa-widget.js\",\"__v\":0,\"content\":\"(function
